### Q3. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 1)

**Options:**

- **(A)** equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
- **(B)** equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
- **(C)** a fallacy
- **(D)** equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></math>**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p>We we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p>Now to solve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></math> ### Q4. JEE Main 2023 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Among the two statements</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is a contradiction and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology</p>

- **(A)** only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> is true

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>The given statement is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Let us take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8745;</mo><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Now let us apply distributive law to the above statement.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8746;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8745;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mo>&#8746;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mi>U</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8745;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mo>&#8746;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mo>&#8746;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now the given statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> will be equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>.</p><p>Hence the given statement is equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>,</p><p>Now negation of given statement by demorgan&#39;s law will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q10. JEE Main 2023 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>~</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>We know that, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p>Now using the above formula we will solve,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p><p>Now finding the negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q11. JEE Main 2023 (06 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Among the statements</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a contradiction</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is True
- **(B)** Both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are True
- **(C)** Only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is True
- **(D)** Only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is True

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is True**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Plotting the Truth table of the given expression we get,</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 500px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>T</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>F</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>F</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>			<td>T</td>		</tr>	</tbody></table><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology, and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is neither tautology and contradiction,</p><p>So both statements are false.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q12. JEE Main 2023 (06 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is logically equivalent to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>We know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p>Hence this is the correct option.</p></blockquote>

</details>

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mi>p</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mi>p</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p>Now solving all options one by one we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>f</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>D</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology</p>

### Q14. JEE Main 2023 (01 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The negation of the expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equivalent to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q15. JEE Main 2023 (31 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> for which <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology, is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given condition is, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>We know conditional disjunction as,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Using De Morgan&#39;s law</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> can be written as,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>For above statement to be a tautology,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> can be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>When <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Two value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> are possible</p>

</details>

### Q16. JEE Main 2023 (31 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Consider:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a contradiction.</p><p>Then</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is correct
- **(B)** both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are correct
- **(C)** both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are wrong
- **(D)** only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is correct

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is correct**

### Q17. JEE Main 2023 (30 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following statements:
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> : I have fever
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> : I will not take medicine
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> : I will take rest
The statement &quot;If I have fever, then I will take medicine and I will take rest&quot; is equivalent to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know the conditional statement:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mtext>if</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>then</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>is&#160;given&#160;by</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p>So the given statements If I have fever, then I will take medicine and I will take rest can be written as,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q18. JEE Main 2023 (30 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Among the statements:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>   is a tautology
- **(B)** Neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> nor <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology
- **(C)** Only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology
- **(D)** Both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> are tautologies

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> nor <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We have,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>If</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>is</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>is</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>is</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math>, then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>T</mi></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>F</mi></math></p><p>then ### Q20. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> are three propositions, then which of the following combination of truth values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> makes the logical expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>{</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>}</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>)</mo></math> false ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, </p><p><math ### Q22. JEE Main 2023 (25 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** a tautology
- **(C)** equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** a contradiction

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) a tautology**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Using logical equivalence condition <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, given statement can be expressed as, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>We know De Morgan&#39;s law of logical equivalence is,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Applying De Morgan&#39;s law in equation(1) we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math> (tautology)</p>

</details>

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> be two statements. Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equivalent to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mtext>as</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mtext>as</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p>

### Q27. JEE Main 2022 (28 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Let
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">p</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramesh listens to music.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">q</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramesh is out of his village
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">r</mi><mo>:</mo></math> It is Sunday
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">s</mi><mo mathvariant="bold">:</mo></math> It is Saturday</p><p>Then the statement &quot;Ramesh listens to music only if he is in his village and it is Sunday or Saturday&quot; can be expressed as</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">p</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramesh listens to music.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">q</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramesh is out of his village
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">r</mi><mo>:</mo></math> It is Sunday
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">s</mi><mo mathvariant="bold">:</mo></math> It is Saturday</p><p>Then the statement &quot;Ramesh listens to music only if he is in his village and it is Sunday or Saturday&quot; can be expressed as</p><p>Now on breaking each statement and combining them we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo></math> Ramesh listens to music</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo></math> He is in village.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo></math> Saturday or Sunday</p><p>So, on combining all statement we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given, the operations <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>*</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8857;</mo><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>. xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>*</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8857;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8857;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology, then the ordered pair <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>*</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8857;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> is</p><p>We will check each option one by one,</p><p>For option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>put&#160;</mtext><mo>*</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8857;</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>&#923;</mi></math></p><p>We get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math <math open="{"><mtext>where&#160;T&#160;is&#160;tautology</mtext></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>T</mi></math></p><p>  Hence <math ### Q29. JEE Main 2022 (27 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the truth value of the statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math>, then the truth value of which of the following is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math>?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>R</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>We know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is false, when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> is true and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is false</p><p>So, for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be false, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> should be true and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math> is false.</p><p>i.e <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is true, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> is false and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> is false.</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 100px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td><math </p><p>for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is <math ### Q33. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

<p>Consider the following statements:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is intelligent.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is rich.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is not honest.</p><p>The negation of the statement &quot;Ramu is intelligent and honest if and only if Ramu is not rich&quot; can be expressed as:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is intelligent</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is rich</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo>:</mo></math> Ramu is not honest</p><p>Given statement, &quot;Ramu is intelligent and honest if and only if Ramu is not rich&quot;</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p>So, negation of the statement is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>[using <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8660;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>]</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>[by using<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>]</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q35. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Negation of the Boolean statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equivalent to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></math></p><p>So negation of<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>r</mi></math>  is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q36. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8660;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> be such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology. Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8743;</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8744;</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8660;</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Now, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#9651;</mo></math> should be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q38. JEE Main 2022 (28 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>r</mi></math> be three logical statements. Consider the compound statements
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Then, which of the following is NOT true?</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is True, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is True
- **(B)** If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is False, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is False
- **(C)** If<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>  is False, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is True
- **(D)** If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is False, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is False

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) If<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>  is False, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is True**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math></p>

</details>

### Q39. JEE Main 2022 (27 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Which of the following statement is a tautology?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math> is a tautology and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p>So,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> will be a tautology.</p>

</details>

### Q40. JEE Main 2022 (27 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> is equivalent to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p>Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q43. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The negation of the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is logically equivalent to

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>To find negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math> &amp; <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p>Using above formula be get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>A</mi></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q45. JEE Main 2022 (24 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Consider the following statements:
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>: Rishi is a judge.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math>: Rishi is honest.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math>: Rishi is not arrogant.</p><p>The negation of the statement &quot;if Rishi is a judge and he is not arrogant, then he is honest&quot; is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>if Rishi is a judge and he is not arrogant, then he is honest can be written as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p>Now, Negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> {Using De-Morgan&#39;s law}</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>
