### Q46. JEE Main 2022 (24 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The number of choices for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8660;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>, such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is a tautology, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> will be always true then the given statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> will be a tautology.</p><p>Now for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mi>&#916;</mi><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> to be true, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;</mi></math> should be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>or</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo></math></p><p>As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q47. JEE Main 2021 (01 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is equivalent to the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Which of the following is equivalent to the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>?</mo></math></p><p>For option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8802;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p>For Option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8802;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>For option <math ### Q50. JEE Main 2021 (27 Aug Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equivalent to:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Case: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math> when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>T</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>F</mi></math></p><p>Statement<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is false as well as option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is also false
option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo></math> option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> are True so given expression is not equivalent to option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> may be true if we consider <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is also not equivalent to option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> Case : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mi>I</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo></math>False</p>

</details>

### Q52. JEE Main 2021 (26 Aug Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the two statements :
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is a fallacy.
Then :

**Options:**

- **(A)** only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> is true.
- **(B)** both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> are false.
- **(C)** only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> is true.
- **(D)** both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> are true.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math> are true.**

**Detailed Solution:**

### Q54. JEE Main 2021 (27 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is the negation of the statement &quot;for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><msup><mi>M</mi><mrow><mo>'</mo><mo>'</mo></mrow></msup><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>M</mi></math> for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math>
- **(B)** there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mi>M</mi></math>
- **(C)** there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>M</mi></math>
- **(D)** there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mi>M</mi></math> for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>M</mi></math> for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo></math> for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mi>M</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo></math> there exists <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>S</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>m</mi></math> </p><p>Negation of &#39;there exsits&#39; is &#39;for all&#39;.</p>

</details>

equivalent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8660;</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p>

### Q56. JEE Main 2021 (25 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Consider the statement &quot;The match will be played only if the weather is good and ground is not wet&quot;. Select the correct negation from the following:

**Options:**

- **(A)** The match will not be played and weather is not good and ground is wet.
- **(B)** If the match will not be played, then either weather is not good or ground is wet.
- **(C)** The match will be played and weather is not good or ground is wet.
- **(D)** The match will not be played or weather is good and ground is not wet.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) The match will be played and weather is not good or ground is wet.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>:</mo></math> weather is good
and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>:</mo></math> ground is not wet</p><p>Given statement &quot;The match will be played only if the weather is good and ground is not wet&quot; can be written as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, negation of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>which is equivalent to &quot;The match will be played and weather is not good or ground is wet&quot;.</p>

</details>

---

### Q57. JEE Main 2021 (25 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equivalent to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>{</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>}</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>  {commutative property}</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>   {distributive property}</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q58. JEE Main 2021 (22 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following Boolean expressions is not a tautology?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>By applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Since, the operation disjunction is commutative, hence, we have</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Now, applying the distributive law i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Also, we know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi></math> represents tautology.</p><p>Thus, we have</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, the option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> is a tautology.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Again, using the same definitions, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, the option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> is also a tautology.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, the option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> is also a tautology.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>4</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology.</p>

</details>

### Q59. JEE Main 2021 (20 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Consider the following three statements:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> then earth is flat.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> If both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> are true then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Then, which of the following statements is correct?</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> is false, but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> are true
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> are true while <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> is false
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> is true while <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> are false
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> are false while <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> is true

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> are true while <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> is false**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Truth table for if-then statement is</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 500px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> (false), and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> (false), therefore <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> is true.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> (true) and earth is flat (false), hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is false.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>C</mi></mfenced></math> Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is false and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math> (false), hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> is true.</p>

</details>

### Q60. JEE Main 2021 (20 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equivalent to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We have,</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 500px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mpadded lspace="-1px"><mi>q</mi></mpadded></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

### Q61. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> are two statements, then which of the following compound statement is a tautology?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>P</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>LHS of all the options are same i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p>(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo></math> tautology</p><p>(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3686277a-5ac3-4005-a63a-b0fbcc4cd3c5-image.png" style="width: 366px; height: 121px;" /></p><p>(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>P</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo></math> Tautology</p><p>(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8800;</mo></math> Tautology</p>

</details>

### Q62. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 1)

<p>The missing value in the following figure is</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/83cb36aa-e807-4068-a72d-83e28a42d2b6-image.png" style="width: 211px; height: 215px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/91d76603-0d22-4530-88b6-ebaba4fb83f8-image.png" style="width: 174px; height: 176px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>w</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>24</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>6</mn></msup></math></p><p>hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q63. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8859;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8855;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8859;</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8855;</mo></math> are respectively given by

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>We know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#10230;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Using De Morgan&#39;s Law i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Using associative law and commutative law i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p>Option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology.</p><p>Option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology.</p><p>Option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>Which is not a tautology.</p><p>Hence, the correct answer is option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q64. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8660;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>*</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math> is a tautology, then the Boolean expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>*</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equivalent to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>*</mo><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>v</mi></math></p><p>Thus, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>*</mo></msup><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>p</mi><mi>v</mi><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2021 (26 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> be two logical expressions. Then :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is a tautology but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is not a tautology
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is not a tautology but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is a tautology
- **(C)** Both <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are not tautologies
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> both are tautologies

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is not a tautology but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is a tautology**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p><math ### Q67. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The contrapositive of the statement &quot;If you will work, you will earn money&quot; is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** If you will not earn money, you will not work
- **(B)** To earn money, you need to work
- **(C)** You will earn money, if you will not work
- **(D)** If you will earn money, you will work

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) If you will not earn money, you will not work**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Constrapositive of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> If you will not earn money, you will not work.</p><p>option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q68. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equivalent to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8596;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>T</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>T</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q70. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The negation of the statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><munder><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#9183;</mo></munder><mi>t</mi></munder><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q71. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The statement among the following that is a tautology is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>T</mi></math></p>

### Q72. JEE Main 2020 (06 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the statement: &quot;For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is odd&quot;. The contrapositive statement of this statement is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is odd.
- **(B)** For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is not even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is not odd.
- **(C)** For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is even.
- **(D)** For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is odd, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is even.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is odd.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>:</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math> is even, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> is odd</p><p>contrapositive of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> &quot;if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is not odd then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math> is not even&quot;</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>For an integer <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is even, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is odd.</p>

</details>

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The negation of the Boolean expression  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equivalent to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given statement is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Negation is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

</details>

- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Negation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8596;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8596;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> (using De&#39;Morgan&#39;s laws)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q76. JEE Main 2020 (04 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Contrapositive of the statement :</p><p>&#39;If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is also continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>&#39;, is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.
- **(B)** If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is not continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.
- **(C)** If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is not continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>. then it is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.
- **(D)** If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is not continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>By using definition, we have;</p><p>Contrapositive of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></math>.</p><p>Which means contrapositive of given statement is;</p><p>&quot;If a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is not continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>, then it is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>.&quot;</p>

</details>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>:</mo><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8596;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a fallacy. ### Q78. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi></math> be three statements such that the truth value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> . Then the truth values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi></math> are respectively :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given that the truth value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> .</p><p>For the above truth value to be possible, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> should be TRUE and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> should be FALSE.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is TRUE when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> both are TRUE.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is FALSE when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> is TRUE and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> is FALSE.</p><p>So, the truth values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>F</mi></math> respectively.</p>

</details>

### Q79. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The proposition <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equivalent to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> By De Morgan&#39;s Laws.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q80. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Which of the following is a tautology?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mo>~</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math>   (where <math ### Q81. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The contrapositive of the statement &quot;If I reach the station in time, then I will catch the train&quot; is

**Options:**

- **(A)** If I do not reach the station in time, then I will catch the train.
- **(B)** If do not reach the station in time, then I will not catch the train.
- **(C)** If I will catch the train, then I reach the station in time.
- **(D)** If I will not catch the train, then I do not reach the station in time.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) If I will not catch the train, then I do not reach the station in time.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Contrapositive of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>q</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>The statement will be &quot;If I will not catch the train, then I do not reach the station in time&quot;.</p>

</details>

---

### Q82. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is false, then the truth values of <math><mi>p</mi></math> and <math><mi>q</mi></math> are respectively

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>F</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<table border="1px solid">	<thead>		<tr>			<th><math><mi>p</mi></math></th>			<th><math><mi>q</mi></math></th>			<th><math><mo>&#8765;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></th>			<th><math><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>&#8765;</mo><mi>q</mi></math></th>			<th><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>&#10230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>&#8765;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></th>		</tr>	</thead>	<tbody>		<tr>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>F</mi></math></td>			<td><math><mi>T</mi></math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table>

### Q83. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Negation of the statement: <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is an integer or <math><mn>5</mn></math> is irrational is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is not an integer <math><mn>5</mn></math> is not irrational
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is not an integer and <math><mn>5</mn></math> is not irrational
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is irrational or <math><mn>5</mn></math> is an integer
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is an integer and <math><mn>5</mn></math> irrational

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is not an integer and <math><mn>5</mn></math> is not irrational**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>~</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8744;</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8743;</mo><mo>~</mo><mi>q</mi></math>, we get negation of given statement as <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> is not an integer and <math><mn>5</mn></math> is not irrational Number.

</details>
